+#!/bin/sh
+# Builds dist/ — the ONLY thing Netlify publishes for Studio Hub.
+#
+# netlify.toml used to say publish = "." and deploys ran from the repo folder, so
+# EVERY file on disk went public — tracked or not: setup/migration-log.txt (staff
+# names + Firebase uids), firebase-agent-defense-hardening.md, CLAUDE.md, the rules,
+# test scripts, firestore-debug.log, even node_modules/ (found Sep 30 2026). This is a
+# pure copy of an explicit allow-list: no transformation, so every deployed byte is
+# a byte of the committed source.
+set -eu
+cd "$(dirname "$0")/.."
+rm -rf dist
+mkdir -p dist
+cp index.html dist/
+cp -R assets css js shared dist/
+echo "[build-dist] dist/: $(find dist -type f | wc -l | tr -d ' ') files (index.html, assets/, css/, js/, shared/)"
diff --git a/scripts/check-live.sh b/scripts/check-live.sh
new file mode 100755
index 0000000..60328f5
--- /dev/null
+++ b/scripts/check-live.sh
@@ -0,0 +1,33 @@
+#!/bin/sh
+# After a deploy: every file in dist/ must be byte-identical on the live site, and
+# paths that must never be public must not serve their own content. Studio Hub's
+# netlify.toml sends every unknown path to index.html (200), so "gone" means: 404, or
+# the body IS index.html. Exit 1 on any mismatch.
+set -u
+cd "$(dirname "$0")/.."
+BASE=https://tinker-hq.netlify.app
+fail=0
+# Complete, not just faithful: every local file index.html, the JS or the CSS references
+# must be IN dist/ (a missing one would fall through to index.html live yet pass below).
+for ref in $( (grep -oE '(src|href)="[^"#:]+"' dist/index.html | sed -E 's/^(src|href)="//; s/"$//'; \
+              grep -ohE "['\"](assets|css|js|shared)/[^'\"]+['\"]" dist/js/*.js | tr -d "'\""; \
+              grep -ohE 'url\([^)]+\)' dist/css/*.css | sed -E "s/^url\(['\"]?//; s/['\"]?\)$//" | grep -v '^data:\|^https\?:') | sed 's/?.*//' | sort -u); do
+  if [ -f "dist/$ref" ]; then echo "  REF    $ref"; else echo "  MISSING $ref   ← referenced but not in dist/"; fail=1; fi
+done
+for f in $(cd dist && find . -type f | sed 's#^\./##'); do
+  a=$(shasum < "dist/$f" | cut -c1-40)
+  b=$(curl -s "$BASE/$f?nocache=$(date +%s)" | shasum | cut -c1-40)
+  if [ "$a" = "$b" ]; then echo "  MATCH  $f"; else echo "  DIFF   $f"; fail=1; fi
+done
+INDEX=$(shasum < dist/index.html | cut -c1-40)
+for p in CLAUDE.md AGENTS.md firebase-agent-defense-hardening.md setup/migration-log.txt setup/create-users.js \
+         firestore.rules storage.rules firebase.json .firebaserc firestore-debug.log package.json rules.test.js \
+         test-alerts.js test-alerts-browser.html TESTING-GUIDE.md scripts/deploy-rules.sh node_modules/.package-lock.json \
+         seasonal-preview.html; do
+  tmp=$(mktemp)
+  code=$(curl -s -o "$tmp" -w "%{http_code}" "$BASE/$p?nocache=$(date +%s)")
+  body=$(shasum < "$tmp" | cut -c1-40); rm -f "$tmp"
+  if [ "$code" = "404" ] || [ "$body" = "$INDEX" ]; then echo "  GONE   $p"; else echo "  $code    $p   ← must not be public"; fail=1; fi
+done
+[ $fail -eq 0 ] && echo "[check-live] ok" || echo "[check-live] FAILED"
+exit $fail
diff --git a/scripts/deploy.sh b/scripts/deploy.sh

+#!/bin/sh
+# The Studio Hub Netlify deploy ritual in one place (~/.claude/CLAUDE.md → NETLIFY).
+# ASK CHRISTIE FIRST — every Netlify deploy costs credits. This script does not ask.
+#
+#   1. refuse unless the tree is clean and HEAD is on the remote
+#   2. build dist/ (pure copy of an allow-list)   3. deploy ONLY dist/ with the full sha as the message
+#   4. check the live site: every deployed file byte-identical, private paths gone
+set -eu
+cd "$(dirname "$0")/.."
+if [ -n "$(git status --porcelain)" ]; then echo "[deploy] refusing: working tree is not clean"; git status --short; exit 1; fi
+git fetch -q
+if [ -n "$(git rev-list @{u}..HEAD)" ]; then echo "[deploy] refusing: HEAD has commits not on the remote — push first"; exit 1; fi
+SHA=$(git rev-parse HEAD)
+sh scripts/build-dist.sh
+netlify deploy --prod --dir dist --message "$SHA"
+sh scripts/check-live.sh
#!/bin/sh
# Builds dist/ — the ONLY thing Netlify publishes for Studio Hub.
#
# netlify.toml used to say publish = "." and deploys ran from the repo folder, so
# EVERY file on disk went public — tracked or not: setup/migration-log.txt (staff
# names + Firebase uids), firebase-agent-defense-hardening.md, CLAUDE.md, the rules,
# test scripts, firestore-debug.log, even node_modules/ (found Sep 30 2026). This is a
# pure copy of an explicit allow-list: no transformation, so every deployed byte is
# a byte of the committed source.
set -eu
cd "$(dirname "$0")/.."
rm -rf dist
mkdir -p dist
cp index.html dist/
cp -R assets css js shared dist/
echo "[build-dist] dist/: $(find dist -type f | wc -l | tr -d ' ') files (index.html, assets/, css/, js/, shared/)"
#!/bin/sh
# The Studio Hub Netlify deploy ritual in one place (~/.claude/CLAUDE.md → NETLIFY).
# ASK CHRISTIE FIRST — every Netlify deploy costs credits. This script does not ask.
#
#   1. refuse unless the tree is clean and HEAD is on the remote
#   2. build dist/ (pure copy of an allow-list)   3. deploy ONLY dist/ with the full sha as the message
#   4. check the live site: every deployed file byte-identical, private paths gone
set -eu
cd "$(dirname "$0")/.."
if [ -n "$(git status --porcelain)" ]; then echo "[deploy] refusing: working tree is not clean"; git status --short; exit 1; fi
git fetch -q
if [ -n "$(git rev-list @{u}..HEAD)" ]; then echo "[deploy] refusing: HEAD has commits not on the remote — push first"; exit 1; fi
SHA=$(git rev-parse HEAD)
sh scripts/build-dist.sh
netlify deploy --prod --dir dist --message "$SHA"
sh scripts/check-live.sh
#!/bin/sh
# After a deploy: every file in dist/ must be byte-identical on the live site, and
# paths that must never be public must not serve their own content. Studio Hub's
# netlify.toml sends every unknown path to index.html (200), so "gone" means: 404, or
# the body IS index.html. Exit 1 on any mismatch.
set -u
cd "$(dirname "$0")/.."
BASE=https://tinker-hq.netlify.app
fail=0
# Complete, not just faithful: every local file index.html, the JS or the CSS references
# must be IN dist/ (a missing one would fall through to index.html live yet pass below).
for ref in $( (grep -oE '(src|href)="[^"#:]+"' dist/index.html | sed -E 's/^(src|href)="//; s/"$//'; \
              grep -ohE "['\"](assets|css|js|shared)/[^'\"]+['\"]" dist/js/*.js | tr -d "'\""; \
              grep -ohE 'url\([^)]+\)' dist/css/*.css | sed -E "s/^url\(['\"]?//; s/['\"]?\)$//" | grep -v '^data:\|^https\?:') | sed 's/?.*//' | sort -u); do
  if [ -f "dist/$ref" ]; then echo "  REF    $ref"; else echo "  MISSING $ref   ← referenced but not in dist/"; fail=1; fi
done
for f in $(cd dist && find . -type f | sed 's#^\./##'); do
  a=$(shasum < "dist/$f" | cut -c1-40)
  b=$(curl -s "$BASE/$f?nocache=$(date +%s)" | shasum | cut -c1-40)
  if [ "$a" = "$b" ]; then echo "  MATCH  $f"; else echo "  DIFF   $f"; fail=1; fi
done
INDEX=$(shasum < dist/index.html | cut -c1-40)
for p in CLAUDE.md AGENTS.md firebase-agent-defense-hardening.md setup/migration-log.txt setup/create-users.js \
         firestore.rules storage.rules firebase.json .firebaserc firestore-debug.log package.json rules.test.js \
         test-alerts.js test-alerts-browser.html TESTING-GUIDE.md scripts/deploy-rules.sh node_modules/.package-lock.json \
         seasonal-preview.html; do
  tmp=$(mktemp)
  code=$(curl -s -o "$tmp" -w "%{http_code}" "$BASE/$p?nocache=$(date +%s)")
  body=$(shasum < "$tmp" | cut -c1-40); rm -f "$tmp"
  if [ "$code" = "404" ] || [ "$body" = "$INDEX" ]; then echo "  GONE   $p"; else echo "  $code    $p   ← must not be public"; fail=1; fi
done
[ $fail -eq 0 ] && echo "[check-live] ok" || echo "[check-live] FAILED"
exit $fail
[build]
  publish = "dist"   # built by scripts/build-dist.sh — an allow-list; never the repo root (Sep 30 2026)

[[redirects]]
  from = "/*"
  to = "/index.html"
  status = 200

#!/bin/sh
# Builds dist/ — the ONLY thing Netlify publishes.
#
# Netlify used to publish the repo root, so the Feb 2026 account-setup pages (one listed
# staff emails with temporary passwords), the test harness, playwright-report/ and
# CLAUDE.md/AGENTS.md were all public. This is a pure copy of an explicit allow-list:
# no transformation, so every deployed byte is a byte of the committed source.
set -eu
cd "$(dirname "$0")/.."
rm -rf dist
mkdir -p dist
cp index.html dist/
cp -R assets css js dist/
echo "[build-dist] dist/: $(find dist -type f | wc -l | tr -d ' ') files (index.html, assets/, css/, js/)"

#!/bin/sh
# The deploy ritual in one place (see CLAUDE.md → Deployment; ~/.claude/CLAUDE.md → NETLIFY).
# ASK CHRISTIE FIRST — every Netlify deploy costs credits. This script does not ask.
#
#   1. refuse unless the tree is clean and HEAD is on the remote
#   2. build dist/ (pure copy)            3. deploy dist/ with the full sha as the message
#   4. check the live site: every deployed file byte-identical, dev paths 404
set -eu
cd "$(dirname "$0")/.."
if [ -n "$(git status --porcelain)" ]; then echo "[deploy] refusing: working tree is not clean"; git status --short; exit 1; fi
git fetch -q
if [ -n "$(git rev-list @{u}..HEAD)" ]; then echo "[deploy] refusing: HEAD has commits not on the remote — push first"; exit 1; fi
SHA=$(git rev-parse HEAD)
sh scripts/build-dist.sh
netlify deploy --prod --dir dist --message "$SHA"
sh scripts/check-live.sh

#!/bin/sh
# After a deploy: every file in dist/ must be byte-identical on the live site, and
# paths that must never be public must 404. Exit 1 on any mismatch.
set -u
cd "$(dirname "$0")/.."
BASE=https://tinker-classbook.netlify.app
fail=0
# Complete, not just faithful: every local file index.html, the JS or the CSS references must
# be IN dist/ (a missing one would 404 live yet pass the byte comparison below).
for ref in $( (grep -oE '(src|href)="[^"#:]+"' dist/index.html | sed -E 's/^(src|href)="//; s/"$//'; \
              grep -ohE "['\"](assets|css|js)/[^'\"]+['\"]" dist/js/*.js | tr -d "'\""; \
              grep -ohE 'url\([^)]+\)' dist/css/*.css | sed -E "s/^url\(['\"]?//; s/['\"]?\)$//" | grep -v '^data:\|^https\?:') | sed 's/?.*//' | sort -u); do
  if [ -f "dist/$ref" ]; then echo "  REF    $ref"; else echo "  MISSING $ref   ← referenced but not in dist/"; fail=1; fi
done
for f in $(cd dist && find . -type f | sed 's#^\./##'); do
  a=$(shasum < "dist/$f" | cut -c1-40)
  b=$(curl -s "$BASE/$f?nocache=$(date +%s)" | shasum | cut -c1-40)
  if [ "$a" = "$b" ]; then echo "  MATCH  $f"; else echo "  DIFF   $f"; fail=1; fi
done
for p in create-users.html user-admin.html fix-user-docs.html create-missing-user-docs.html create-auth-accounts.js \
         AGENTS.md CLAUDE.md CLASSBOOK-DATA-SAFETY-PLAN.md PROJECT-SUMMARY.md SETUP-USER-DOCS.md \
         package.json package-lock.json playwright.config.js e2e/README.md playwright-report/index.html scripts/deploy.sh; do
  code=$(curl -s -o /dev/null -w "%{http_code}" "$BASE/$p")
  if [ "$code" = "404" ]; then echo "  404    $p"; else echo "  $code    $p   ← must not be public"; fail=1; fi
done
[ $fail -eq 0 ] && echo "[check-live] ok" || echo "[check-live] FAILED"
exit $fail

exec
/bin/zsh -lc "find index.html assets css js shared -type f -print | sort && rg -n --no-heading \"(?:src|href)=|url\\(|fetch\\(|import\\(|new URL|serviceWorker|manifest|assets/|css/|js/|shared/|\\.html|\\.json|\\.png|\\.svg|\\.jpg|\\.jpeg|\\.webp|\\.gif|\\.ico|\\.woff|\\.ttf|\\.mp3|\\.wav|\\.pdf\" index.html js css shared --glob '"'!*.map'"'" in /Users/christiehubley/studio-hub/.claude/worktrees/classbook-alerts-own-doc

// =====================================================
// Studio Hub - Main Application Logic
// =====================================================

// State
let currentUser = null;
let currentFilter = 'all';
let firebaseInitialized = false;

// Utility: capitalize first letter of each word
function capitalizeName(name) {
  return name ? name.split(' ').map(w => w.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + w.slice(1)).join(' ') : '';
}

// Utility: Get time-based greeting
function getTimeBasedGreeting() {
  const hour = new Date().getHours();
  if (hour < 12) return { text: 'Good morning', emoji: '☀️' };
  if (hour < 17) return { text: 'Good afternoon', emoji: '🌤️' };
  if (hour < 21) return { text: 'Good evening', emoji: '🌙' };
  return { text: 'Good night', emoji: '✨' };
}

// Add floating shapes to page
function addFloatingShapes() {
  const container = document.createElement('div');
  container.className = 'floating-shapes';

  const shapes = [
    'Asset 1.svg',
    'Asset 2.svg',
    'Asset 3.svg',
    'Asset 5.svg',
    'Asset 6.svg',
    'Asset 8.svg',
    'Asset 10.svg',
    'Asset 12.svg',
    'Asset 13.svg',
    'Asset 14.svg'
  ];

  const sizes = ['small', 'large', 'xlarge', 'large', 'small', 'xlarge', 'large', 'small', 'large', 'xlarge'];

  shapes.forEach((shape, i) => {
    const shapeEl = document.createElement('img');
    shapeEl.src = `assets/shapes/${shape}`;
    shapeEl.className = `floating-shape ${sizes[i]}`;
    container.appendChild(shapeEl);
  });

  document.body.appendChild(container);
}

// DOM Elements
const loginScreen = document.getElementById('login-screen');
const hubContainer = document.getElementById('hub-container');
const loginForm = document.getElementById('login-form');
const loginError = document.getElementById('login-error');
const departmentsContainer = document.getElementById('departments-container');
const filterButtons = document.querySelectorAll('.filter-btn');
const userMenuBtn = document.getElementById('user-menu-btn');
const userDropdown = document.getElementById('user-dropdown');
const logoutBtn = document.getElementById('logout-btn');

// =====================================================
// Demo Mode (No Firebase Required)
// =====================================================

// Demo users for testing without Firebase
const DEMO_USERS = {
  'admin@studio.com': {
    password: 'admin123',
    name: 'Studio Admin',
    email: 'admin@studio.com',
    role: 'admin',
    studios: ['tinker', 'clayhub']
  },
  'manager@studio.com': {
    password: 'manager123',
    name: 'Manager',
    email: 'manager@studio.com',
    role: 'manager',
    studios: ['tinker', 'clayhub']
  },
  'staff@studio.com': {
    password: 'staff123',
    name: 'Staff Member',
    email: 'staff@studio.com',
    role: 'staff',
    studios: ['tinker']
  }
};

// =====================================================
// Authentication
// =====================================================

function initAuth() {
  // Check if Firebase is configured
  if (CONFIG.firebase.apiKey !== 'YOUR_API_KEY') {
    // Initialize Firebase
    try {
      firebase.initializeApp(CONFIG.firebase);
      firebaseInitialized = true;

      // Listen for auth state changes
      firebase.auth().onAuthStateChanged(handleAuthStateChange);
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Firebase init error:', error);
      initDemoMode();
    }
  } else {
    // Use demo mode
    initDemoMode();
  }
}

function initDemoMode() {
  console.log('Running in demo mode. Demo accounts:');
  console.log('- admin@studio.com / admin123 (Admin)');
  console.log('- manager@studio.com / manager123 (Manager)');
  console.log('- staff@studio.com / staff123 (Staff)');

  // Check for saved demo session
  const savedUser = localStorage.getItem('studioHubDemoUser');
  if (savedUser) {
    currentUser = JSON.parse(savedUser);
    showHub();
  } else {
    showLogin();
  }
}

async function handleAuthStateChange(user) {
  if (user) {
    // Fetch user data from Firestore
    try {
      const db = firebase.firestore();
      const userDoc = await db.collection('users').doc(user.uid).get();
      if (userDoc.exists) {
        currentUser = {
          uid: user.uid,
          email: user.email,
          ...userDoc.data()
        };
      } else {
        // First login — create default user doc in Firestore
        const defaultUser = {
          name: user.displayName || user.email.split('@')[0],
          email: user.email,
          role: 'staff',
          studios: ['tinker', 'clayhub'],
          appAccess: [],
          createdAt: new Date().toISOString()
        };
        await db.collection('users').doc(user.uid).set(defaultUser);
        console.log('Created new user doc for', user.email);
        currentUser = { uid: user.uid, ...defaultUser };
      }
      showHub();
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Error fetching user data:', error);
      currentUser = {
        uid: user.uid,
        email: user.email,
        name: user.displayName || 'User',
        role: 'staff',
        studios: ['tinker', 'clayhub']
      };
      showHub();
    }
  } else {
    currentUser = null;
    showLogin();
  }
}

 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	#!/bin/sh
     2	# Builds dist/ — the ONLY thing Netlify publishes for Studio Hub.
     3	#
     4	# netlify.toml used to say publish = "." and deploys ran from the repo folder, so
     5	# EVERY file on disk went public — tracked or not: setup/migration-log.txt (staff
     6	# names + Firebase uids), firebase-agent-defense-hardening.md, CLAUDE.md, the rules,
     7	# test scripts, firestore-debug.log, even node_modules/ (found Sep 30 2026). This is a
     8	# pure copy of an explicit allow-list: no transformation, so every deployed byte is
     9	# a byte of the committed source.
    10	set -eu
    11	cd "$(dirname "$0")/.."
    12	rm -rf dist
    13	mkdir -p dist
    14	cp index.html dist/
    15	cp -R assets css js shared dist/
    16	echo "[build-dist] dist/: $(find dist -type f | wc -l | tr -d ' ') files (index.html, assets/, css/, js/, shared/)"
     1	#!/bin/sh
     2	# After a deploy: every file in dist/ must be byte-identical on the live site, and
     3	# paths that must never be public must not serve their own content. Studio Hub's
     4	# netlify.toml sends every unknown path to index.html (200), so "gone" means: 404, or
     5	# the body IS index.html. Exit 1 on any mismatch.
     6	set -u
     7	cd "$(dirname "$0")/.."
     8	BASE=https://tinker-hq.netlify.app
     9	fail=0
    10	# Complete, not just faithful: every local file index.html, the JS or the CSS references
    11	# must be IN dist/ (a missing one would fall through to index.html live yet pass below).
    12	for ref in $( (grep -oE '(src|href)="[^"#:]+"' dist/index.html | sed -E 's/^(src|href)="//; s/"$//'; \
    13	              grep -ohE "['\"](assets|css|js|shared)/[^'\"]+['\"]" dist/js/*.js | tr -d "'\""; \
    14	              grep -ohE 'url\([^)]+\)' dist/css/*.css | sed -E "s/^url\(['\"]?//; s/['\"]?\)$//" | grep -v '^data:\|^https\?:') | sed 's/?.*//' | sort -u); do
    15	  if [ -f "dist/$ref" ]; then echo "  REF    $ref"; else echo "  MISSING $ref   ← referenced but not in dist/"; fail=1; fi
    16	done
    17	for f in $(cd dist && find . -type f | sed 's#^\./##'); do
    18	  a=$(shasum < "dist/$f" | cut -c1-40)
    19	  b=$(curl -s "$BASE/$f?nocache=$(date +%s)" | shasum | cut -c1-40)
    20	  if [ "$a" = "$b" ]; then echo "  MATCH  $f"; else echo "  DIFF   $f"; fail=1; fi
    21	done
    22	INDEX=$(shasum < dist/index.html | cut -c1-40)
    23	for p in CLAUDE.md AGENTS.md firebase-agent-defense-hardening.md setup/migration-log.txt setup/create-users.js \
    24	         firestore.rules storage.rules firebase.json .firebaserc firestore-debug.log package.json rules.test.js \
    25	         test-alerts.js test-alerts-browser.html TESTING-GUIDE.md scripts/deploy-rules.sh node_modules/.package-lock.json \
    26	         seasonal-preview.html; do
    27	  tmp=$(mktemp)
    28	  code=$(curl -s -o "$tmp" -w "%{http_code}" "$BASE/$p?nocache=$(date +%s)")
    29	  body=$(shasum < "$tmp" | cut -c1-40); rm -f "$tmp"
    30	  if [ "$code" = "404" ] || [ "$body" = "$INDEX" ]; then echo "  GONE   $p"; else echo "  $code    $p   ← must not be public"; fail=1; fi
    31	done
    32	[ $fail -eq 0 ] && echo "[check-live] ok" || echo "[check-live] FAILED"
    33	exit $fail
     1	#!/bin/sh
     2	# The Studio Hub Netlify deploy ritual in one place (~/.claude/CLAUDE.md → NETLIFY).
     3	# ASK CHRISTIE FIRST — every Netlify deploy costs credits. This script does not ask.
     4	#
     5	#   1. refuse unless the tree is clean and HEAD is on the remote
     6	#   2. build dist/ (pure copy of an allow-list)   3. deploy ONLY dist/ with the full sha as the message
     7	#   4. check the live site: every deployed file byte-identical, private paths gone
     8	set -eu
     9	cd "$(dirname "$0")/.."
    10	if [ -n "$(git status --porcelain)" ]; then echo "[deploy] refusing: working tree is not clean"; git status --short; exit 1; fi
    11	git fetch -q
    12	if [ -n "$(git rev-list @{u}..HEAD)" ]; then echo "[deploy] refusing: HEAD has commits not on the remote — push first"; exit 1; fi
    13	SHA=$(git rev-parse HEAD)
    14	sh scripts/build-dist.sh
    15	netlify deploy --prod --dir dist --message "$SHA"
    16	sh scripts/check-live.sh
     1	[build]
     2	  publish = "dist"   # built by scripts/build-dist.sh — an allow-list; never the repo root (Sep 30 2026)
     3	
     4	[[redirects]]
     5	  from = "/*"
     6	  to = "/index.html"
     7	  status = 200

## Verdict: NOT SAFE TO DEPLOY

Current app contents are complete: all 51 runtime files, dynamically selected icons/shapes, CSS, and shared files are included. No service worker or manifest exists, and other apps only link to the HQ root.

Minimum fixes:

1. [build-dist.sh](/Users/christiehubley/studio-hub/.claude/worktrees/classbook-alerts-own-doc/scripts/build-dist.sh:15) recursively copies ignored/untracked files inside `assets/`, `css/`, `js/`, or `shared/`. The clean-tree guard does not detect ignored files, so private content can still be published. Build from tracked files or reject anything untracked/ignored in these directories.

2. [check-live.sh](/Users/christiehubley/studio-hub/.claude/worktrees/classbook-alerts-own-doc/scripts/check-live.sh:17) splits filenames on spaces. Existing files such as `assets/shapes/Asset 1.svg` make the post-deploy check fail. Its reference scan also misses the dynamically constructed shape paths at [app.js](/Users/christiehubley/studio-hub/.claude/worktrees/classbook-alerts-own-doc/js/app.js:46), so its claimed completeness guarantee is unsound. The SPA-fallback body comparison itself is correct.

3. [deploy.sh](/Users/christiehubley/studio-hub/.claude/worktrees/classbook-alerts-own-doc/scripts/deploy.sh:15) does not pin or verify the Netlify site ID. In a worktree it trusts `NETLIFY_SITE_ID`; a wrong value can deploy `dist/` to another site before the hard-coded HQ check runs.

The dirty-tree and unpushed-commit checks otherwise behave as described. No files were edited and no tests were run.
